[EVENT: IRQ46930]
Date: 2023-06-15
Country: Iraq | Location: Sileen
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Iraq) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Al Mirbad [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 15 June 2023, locals protested in the Sileen marshes area in northern Basrah province. The protesters demanded to know what happened to the financial allocations promised to them under the premiership of Nouri Al Maloki. Malik had promised 30,000,000 Iraqi Dinars (about 23,000 USD) to each family. The protesters demanded the financial allocations be paid in accordance with Article 140. The protesters also demanded the Basrah Oil Company cease operations in their areas and pay compensation to the locals. The protesters threatened to escalated their methods by blocking roads leading to oil fields and boycotting elections if their demands are not met.

[EVENT: IRQ47131]
Date: 2023-06-27
Country: Iraq | Location: Sayyid Dakhil
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Al Ziyad Tribal Militia (Iraq) [Identity militia]
Actor2: Al Hassasra Tribal Militia (Iraq) [Identity militia]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: National Iraqi News Agency; Shafaaq News [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 27 June 2023, Al Hassasra and Al Ziyad tribal militants clashed with medium weapons in Sayyid Dakhil district, Thi Qar. The clash took place after a member of the Al Ziyad tribe took a member of the Al Hassasra tribe to court and one of the two tribes was not happy with the court's decision. The clash injured 1 civilian by-stander. After the clash ended, Thi Qar police began raiding homes in search of those suspected of taking part in the clash and on 27 June arrested 7 suspects. There were no fatalities.
